ORDER 


S . 0 . 318. --- WHEREAS the Election Commission is satisfied 
that Shri S . Chelliah , Naduvikottai Post, Pattukottui Taluk , 
Thanjavur District ( Tamil Nadu ), a contesting candidate for 
election 10 the Tamil Nadu Legislative Assembly from 
190-Peravurani constituency , held in March , 1971 has failed 
to lodge an account of his election expenses in the manner as 
l equired by the Representation of the People Act, 1951, and 
the Rules made thereunder ; 


का . आ . 318.---यतः, निवार्चन आयोग का समाधान हो गया है कि 
मार्च, 1971 को हुए समिलनाह, विधान सभा के निर्वाचन के लिए 
190-पंरावरानी निर्वाचन क्षेत्र से चनाव लड़ने वाले उम्मीदवार 
श्री एस . चल्लीहा नहवी कोट्टाय पोस्ट पट्टकोट्टाय तालक, थंजावर 
जिला ( तमिलनाडा) लोक प्रतिनिधि अधिनियम , 1951 तथा 
तद्धीन बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा अपेक्षित रीति से अपने निर्वाचन 
म्ययों का लेखा साखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं । 
___ और, यतः, उक्त उम्मीदवार ने, उस सम्यक सुचना दिये जाने पर 
भी , अपनी इस असफलता के लिए कई कारण अथवा स्पष्टीकरण 
नहीं दिया है , और निवाचन आयोग का यह भी समाधान हो 
गया है कि उसके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पास कारण 
या न्यायाचित्य नहीं है । 

अतः अम , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10- क के अनुसरण में 
निवाचन आयोग एतद्वारा उक्त श्री एस . चेल्लीहा को संसद 
के किसी भी सदन के या किसी राज्य की विधान - सभा अथवा विधान 
परिषद् के सदस्य चने जाने और होने के लिए इस आदेश की तारीख 
से तीन वर्ष की कालावधि के लिए निरिहत घोषित करता है । 

[ सं . तमिलना - वि , स./180 / 71 (36 ) ] 
42 G of I/ 72 - 1, 


AND WHEREAS, the said candidate even after the duc 
notice has not given any reason or explanation for the failure, 
and the Election Commission is satisfied that he has no good 
reason or justification for the failure ; 


NOW , THEREFORE, in pursuance of section 10A of the 
said Act, the Election Commission hereby declares the sald 
Shri S. Chelliah to be disqualified for being chosen as , and 
for being, a member of either House of Parliament or of the 
Legislative Assembly or Legislative Council of a State for a 
period of three years from the date of this order . 


[ No. TN/ I. A / 190 / 71(36)]

का . आ . 336.--- ककारी विनियमन अधिनियम , 1949 (1949 
का 10 वां ) की धारा 56 के खण्ड ( य ख ) के साथ पठित धारा 45 
की उप - धारा ( 2 ) के द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, 
केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त धारा 45 की उपधारा (1 ) के अन्तर्गत भारतीय 
रिजर्व बैंक नारा दिये गये आवेदन-पत्र पर विचार करने के बाद कल्ना 
कटवा सेन्ट्रल कापरेटिव बैंक लिमिटेड । (जिसे इसके पश्चात 
सहकारी बैंक कहा गया है ) के सम्बन्ध में एतद्वारा 27 जनवरी , 
1973 को बैंक का कारोबार बंद होने से लेकर 30 अप्रैल , 1973 तक 
और इस दिन को मिलाकर, अधिस्थगन आदेश जारी करती है जिसके 
अनुसार अधिस्थगत आदेश की अवधि के दौरान सहकारी बैंक के 
विरुद्ध सभी कार्रवाइयों का शुरू किया जाना अथवा शुरू की गई 
कार्रवाइयों को जारी रखना स्थगित किया जाता है , परन्त शर्त यह 
है कि उस प्रकार का स्थगन किसी भी प्रकार से बंगाल सहकारी संस्था 
अधिनियम , 1940 (1940 का 21 वां बंगाल अधिनियम ) के 
अन्तर्गत पश्चिम बंगाल सरकार द्वारा प्रयोग में लाये जाने वाले 
उसके अधिकारों के प्रसिकल न हो । 

2. केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा यह निर्देश भी देती है कि उस 
स्वीकृत अधिस्थगन की अधि के दौरान सहकारी बैंक भारतीय 
रिजर्व बैंक की लिखित रूप में अनुमति के बिना कोई ग्रहण अथवा 
अग्रिम नहीं देगा, किसी प्रकार का दायित्व स्वीकार नहीं करेगा, 
कोई निवेश नहीं करेगा अथवा अपने दायित्वों और देनदारियों के 
सम्बन्ध में अथवा अन्यथा किसी प्रकार की अदायगी नहीं करेगा 
एवं अदायगी करना स्वीकार नहीं करेगा या किसी प्रकार का समझौता 
अथवा ठहराव नहीं करेगा, किन्त, वह निम्नलिखित तरीके से और 
निम्नलिखित सीमा सक यथास्थिति अदागियां अथवा खर्स कर 
समगा ।

मशत कि अदा की गयी रकम की कछ राशि किसी एक व्यक्ति 

(किसी अन्य क्ष्यक्ति के साथ संयक्त खाते में नहीं ) के 
नाम में खाते में जमा क,ल राशि के 10 प्रतिशत से अधिक 
अथवा 50 रूपये, इनमें जो भी अधिक हो , उससे ज्यादा न 
हो । यह भी शर्त है कि एस किसी यक्ति को कोई रकम 
अदा नहीं की जाएगी जो किसी प्रकार से सहकारी बैंक का 

कर्जदार हो ;

किर जाने है जिस तारीख को स्थगन आदेश लागू होता 


(3 ) 27 जनवरी, 1973 को अथवा उससे पूर्व भुगतान के लिए

का . आ . 339.--- ककारी विनियमन अधिनियग . 1049 (1949 
क 10 वां ) की धारा 56 के खण्ड ( य ख ) के साथ पठित धारा 45 की 
उप - धारा ( 2) के द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय 
सरकार उक्त धारा 45 की उपधारा (1 ) के अन्तर्गत भारतीय रिजर्व 
बैंक द्वारा दियं गए आवेदन -पत्र पर विचार करने के बाद 24 परगना 
सदर्न सेन्ट्रल कोआपरेटिव बैंक लिमिटेड (जिसे इसके पश्चात् सरकारी 
बैंक कहा गया है ) के सम्बन्ध में एतद्वारा 27 जनवरी, 1973 को 
बैंक का कारोबार बन्द होने से लेकर 30 अप्रैल , 1973 तक और इस 
दिन को मिलाकर, अधिस्थगन आदेश जारी करती है जिसके अनुसार 

अधिस्थगन आदेश की अवधि के दौरान सहकारी बैंक के विरुद्ध 
सभी कार्रवाइयों का शुरू किया जाना अथवा शुरू की गयी कार्रवाइयों 
को जारी रखना स्थगित किया जाता है , परन्त , शर्त यह है कि उस 
प्रकार का स्थगन किसी भी प्रकार से बंगाल सरकारी संस्था अधिनियम , 


( 4 ) एसा कोई ध्यय जो किसी सहकारी बैंक के द्वारा अथवा 

उसके विरुद्ध दायर किए गये मुकदमे , अपील , अथवा सहकारी 
बैंक द्वारा ली गयी डिगरी या बैंक को मिलने वाली किसी 
रकम को वसूल करने के सम्बन्ध में करना आवश्यक हो , 
किन्तु यदि ऐसे प्रत्येक मुकदमी , अपील अथवा डिगरी के 
सम्बन्ध में किए जाने वाले त्यय की रकम 250/- रुपये से 
अधिक हो तो खर्च करने से पहले भारतीय रिजर्व बैंक की 

लिखित अनुमति ली जाएगी , और 
( 3) किसी अन्य मद पर कोई व्यय , जहां तक कि वह व्यय 

सहकारी बैंक के विचार में बैंक का दौनक प्रशासन चलाने 
के लिए करना अनिवार्य हो बशतक जहां किसी एक 

लेण्डर मास में किसी मद पर किया गया क, छ खर्च अधि 
स्थगन आदेश से पहले के छः कलण्डर महीनों में उस मद 
पर किए गए औसत मासिक व्यय से बढ जाता हो , अथवा 
जहां उस मद के सम्बन्ध में कोई व्यय नहीं किया गया 
हो और उस प्रकार किया जाने वाले व्यय 500 / - रुपये से 
बन जाए तो उस प्रकार का व्यय करने से पूर्व भारतीय रिजर्व 
बैंक की लिखित रूप में अनुमति ली जाएगी ।

शों के साथ मान्यता प्रदान करती है :

आदेश 


लेटेक्स मैकनिकल स्टबिलिटि टेस्ट अपरेटस तथा इसके स्वीकृत 
फालतू पूर्जा के आयात के लिए 4149 रु. ( चार हजार एक सौ उनचास 
रु. मात्र ) के लिए एक लाइसेंस सं . पी / एस / 1877038/ सी / एक्स 
एक्स / 38/ ई / 31 -32, दिनांक 19- 2- 71 स्वीकृत किया गया था । 

उन्होंने उपयुक्त लाइसेंस की अनलिपि सीमाशुल्क प्रति के 
लिए इस आधार पर आवेदन किया है कि मूल लाइसेंस बिल्कल 
उपयोग किए बिना ही खो गया / अस्थानस्थ हो गया है । अपने 
तर्क के समर्थन में आवेदक में एक शपथ पत्र दाखिल किया है । 

में संतुष्ट है कि लाइसेंस सं . पी / एस / 1877038 / सी / एक्स 
एक्स /38 / ई / 31-32, दिनांक 19-2-71 जो जुलाई 1972 तक पनवैध 
किया गया था उसकी मूल सीमाशुल्क प्रति बिल्कल उपयोग किए 
बिना ही खो गई है और निर्देश देता हूं कि आवेदक को उपयुक्त 
लाइसेंस की अनलिपि सीमा शुल्क प्रति जारी की जानी चाहिए । 

[ संख्या. 2/ 72-73/ ए. यू ./ एस . एस . आई ] 

पी . गोविन्द राजू , उप मुख्य नियंत्रक . 


का . आ . 347: - सप्रिंटीन्हंग इन्जी. यमना सिविल कन्स् 
ट्रक्शन किल देहरादून को मैटसन बीवल गेयर जनरेटिंग अटेच 
मेंट के लिए फालतू पुजो के आयात के लिए एक लाइसौस सं . जी / ए/ 
1031143 दिनांक 17-3-67 स्वीकृत किया गया था । दि समिटन्हिंग 
इन्जी., पमना सिविल कन्स्ट्रक्शन स कल , देहरावन ने यह बताया 
है कि उपयक्त लाइसेंस की सीमाशुल्क तथा मजा विनिमय नियंत्रण 
प्रतियां अस्थानस्थ हो गई है और उन्होंने यह निवेदन किया है। 
कि उपयुक्त लाइसेंस की अनलिपि प्रतियां जारी की जानी चाहिए । 

अपने तर्क के समर्थन में आवेदक ने एक शपथ पत्र दाखिल किया 
है । अधोहस्ताक्षरी इससे संतुष्ट है कि लाइसेंस की सीमाशुल्क तथा 
मद्रा विनिमय नियंत्रण प्रतियां खो गई है और निर्देश देता है 
कि आवेदक को उक्त लाइसेंस की अनलिपि सीमाशुल्क तथा मद्रा 
विनिमय नियंत्रण प्रतियां जारी की जानी चाहिए । 

लाइसेंस की मूल सीमाशुल्क तथा मद्रा विनिमय नियंत्रण प्रतियां 
रन कर दी गई है । इसीकी अनुलिपि प्रतियां अलग से जारी की 
जा रही हैं । 

[ सं . पी आर ओ/ 549 / 88- 67 / एल -1 बी / 1299]

का . आ . 363. - यतः पेट्रोलियम , पाइपलाइन ( भूमि के 
उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1982 ( 1962 का 50 ) 
की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम 
और रसायन तथा खान और धात, मंत्रालय (पेट्रोलियम विभाग ) 
की अधिसूचना का . आ . 1872 तारीख 22-4 -72 इचारा केन्द्रीय 
सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनसूची में विक्टि भूमिओं 
के उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइप लाइनों को बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिए अर्जित करने का अपना आशय घोषित कर दिया था । 

और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी के उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 8 की 
उपधारा (1) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

और आगे , यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने 
के पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनसूची में विनिदिष्ट 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करने का विनिश्चय किया 
है । 

अब , अतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा (1 ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एसद्वारा घोषित 
करती है कि इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिदिष्ट 
उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाइप लाइन बिछाने के 
प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्द्वारा अर्जित किया जाता है । 
___ और, आगे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों 
का प्रयोग करते हए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त भूमियों 
में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में विहित होने के बजाय सेल 
और प्रकृतिक गैस आयोग में सभी बंधकों से मुक्त रूप में , इस घोषणा 
के प्रकाशन की इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

और आगे, यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्स रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के 
पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना सं संलग्न अनुसूची में विदिष्ट भूमियों 
में उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करने का निश्चय किया है । 
___ अब , अत : उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा (1 ) प्यारा 
प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा घोषित 
करती है कि इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिदिष्ट उक्त 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिए एतद्द्वारा अर्जित किया जाता है । 

और, आगे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त क्सियों 
का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निदेश देती है कि उक्त भूमियों 
में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में विहित होने के बजाय 
तेल और प्राकृतिक गैस आयोग में , सभी बंधकों से मुक्त रूप में , 
इस घोषणा के प्रकाशन की इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

laying pipelines ;

का . आ . 378. - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची म " यिनिदिष्ट विषयों के बारे म * पंजाब नेशनल 
* क से सम्बद्ध नियोजिकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक 
औद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है , 
___ और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना यांछनीय समझती है , 

अतः, अब , औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 (1947 का 
14 ) की धारा 7 -क और धारा 10 की उपधारा (1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) 
द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्

निर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 


अनुसूची 
" क्या पंजाब नेशनल बैंक, संन्ट्रल सर्किल , इन्दौर के प्रबंधतंत्र की ,

की कार्रवाई न्यायोचित है ? यदि नहीं , तो वह किस अनतोष 
का हकदार है ? " 

[ सं . एल . 12012 / 44/ 72- एल आर 3 ] 


MINISTRY OF LABOUR AND REHABILITATION 

(Department of Labour and Employment) 


और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

प्रतः प्रब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 19 47 का 14 ) की 
धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्सियों 
का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतवद्वारा उक्त विवाद को उक्त 
अधिनियम की धारा 7 – वा के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण 
कानमत्ता को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 

अनुसूची 1 
“ क्या कोलियरी मजदूर सभा , जी० टी० रोड़, डाकघर 
प्रासनसोल , जिला बर्दवान की यह मांग, कि अनुसूची - 2 में उस्लि 
खित कर्मकार ईस्ट मिचा कोलियरी, हाकपर जेके नगर , 
जिला बर्दबान के प्रबन्धतंत्र द्वारा सेवा में वापस लिये जाने और 
26 दिसम्बर 1971 से पूरे मजदूरी के भुगतान किए जाने के हकदार 
हैं न्यायोचित है यदि हां तो कर्मकार किम अनुतोष के और 
किस तारीख से हकदार हैं ?

New Delhi, the 30th Januory , 1973 


SO . 384 . - In pursuance of section 17 of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Central Government 
hereby publishes the following award of the Industrial Tri 
bunal, Chandigarh in the industrial dispute between the 
employers in relation to the State Bank of Patiala and their 
workmen , which was received by the Central Government 
on the 25th January , 1973. 


It is true that Shri Rattan Lal claims that Shri Jaswant 
Singh had not left any address with the respondent or the 
Personnel Ollicer when he went for military training , but 
the fact remains that Shri Jaswant Singh s home address 
as well as of cvery employee is expected to be found 
in service book . Not only this Shri Jaswant Singh has 
alleged that besides his home address being with the res 
pondent in the scrvice book , he had also given his address 
in the representation that he had filed against the orders 
passed in respect of termination of his services . It is thus 
abundantly clear that the respondents had the address of 
Shri Jaswant Singh but for reasons best known to them 
they did not care to intorm him or direct him to join duity 
in pursuance of the award given in tho earlier complaint 
Aled by Shri Jaswant Singh .

AWARD 


An industrial dispute having arisen between the workmen 
and the management of the State Bank of Patiala , rogarding 
the matter specified below , the Government of India referred 
the same to this Tribunal for adjudication - vide Ministry of 
Labour & Employment and Rehabilitation , Departments (De 
partments of Labour & Employment) Notification No. L . 
12012 / 94 /71 /LR JII, duted 22nd January , 1972 : 


The respondent s authorised representative Shri Rattan 
Lal has urged that since an enquiry had becn held against 
Shri Jaswant Singh as was evident from various documents 
placed on record in that behalf and that on its basis action 
had been taken i.e . as per findings of Enquiry officer ; 
merely because another vicw could be taken the findings 
of Enquiry officers could not be brushed aside unless the 
findings of the Enquiry Officer were perversoor enquiry 
that was held was not shown to have been fair and proper 
or there had been discrimination , victimisation or violation 
of principles of natural justice , and that this Tribunal could 
not take a different view from the one taken . However , 
this argument is without substance as enquiry which was 
held relates to first order of termination on basis of which 
services of Shri Jaswant Singh had been terminated on 
24 - 8 - 1971 and which order was found to be in contraven 
tion with the provisions of sections 33A and 33 ( 2 ) of the 
Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 and there was no material 
relating to any other enquiry on the record or any other 
such proceedings that may have been taken against Shri 
Jaswant Singh before the order , Ext. C / 4 , was passed on 
22- 6 - 1972 . 


Whether the action of the management of State Bank 
of Patiala in terminating the services of Shri Mohinder 
Singh , Armed Guard at their Doraha Branch with effect 
from the 8th May, 1968 is justified ? If not , to what 
relief is he entitled ?

SEC . 3 ( ii) ] 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : FEBRUARY 10 , 1973 /MAGHA 21 , 1894 


555 


SIS 


held on the assumption that he was guilty of the charges 
with regard to the charges levelled until the charges are proy 
ed , and that an enguiry , as in the instant case , had bech 
and liable to i particular punishment, such an enquiry is in 
violation of the principles of natural justice . 


The only question that arises for consideration is whether 
Shri Mohinder Singh should be ordered to be reinstated from 
the date his services were terminated or be allowed compen 
sation instead . Since the respondent bank bave maintained 
that he had lost their confidence and they have in this behalf 
rclied upon A .I.R . 1954 - S . C .-506 and the statoments made hy 
Shri Mohinder Singh , Ext. R / 20 und R /21 which go to show 
the past record and conduct . 


It is unnecessary to go into detail in respect of this aspect 
of the matter as the enquiry officer has exhonerated Shri 
Mohinder Singh of the serious chargc against him , and has 
only found that he had put off Sarvashri Balinder Singh anı 
Bhan Singh when they had gone to deliver a letter to him 
an :1 they have not indicated as to what the contents of thnt 
letter were . 


It has becn maintained on behalf of the respondent bank . 
whilc relying lipon A . I. R .- S . C .- 136 . that it was open to them 
in case of gross misconduct to discharge their cmployee with 
or without notice or payment of three months wages etc . 
When the evidence was found sufficient to cstablish the 
charge and which the bank does not for some reasons think 
it expedient to retain siich a person any longer in service . 


The respondent bank has also relied upon A .I. R .- 1971 
S . C .- 2414 and 1972 -S . C .-1975 for the proposition that where 
an employee was discharging duties of an office of trust and 
his services are terminated on the ground of loss of confidence, 
the Industrial Tribunal should not direct his reinstatement or 
direct that he should be employed in another job . 

The workmen has, on the other hand , relied upon 1957 
Andhra Pradesh -414 , and maintained that neither in the 
charge sheet nor in the show cause notice or while passing 
orders the punishing authority or the appellate authority had 
given any indication that he had lost confidence of the Bank , 


No doubt the Bank is competent to take action mentioned 
in the aforesaid citution , but there has to be sufficient mate 
rial to sustain the charge and the employec s services cannot 
be dispensed within an arbitrary manner. 


According to 1960 - Service Law Reporter -494 payment of 
pay and allowances is a condition precedent to the termins 
tion of services, and in the instant case the Punishing autho 
rity and the appellate authority had ordered that Shri Mobin 
der Singh should be paid three month s pay and allowances 
as wages for the notice period, but no evidence has been 
led to show that such a payment was made . 


The workmen has relied upon a large number of 
citations to show that it was upon the appellate AU 
thority to provide him with opportunity of personal hearing 
which had been denied to him . In a case of termination of 
services under the Indian Customs Act it has been inter alia 
held i.e . in 1957 - A . I.R .- S . C .- 148 that it was not necessary that 
personal hearing be given at every stage. 


It may be stated in this behalf that Shri Mohinder Singh 
was in the employment of the respondent Bank as an armed 
guard which post is no doubt an office of trust and involves 
security of the bank and since the respondent bank claims 
that they have lost confidence in him , it would not be expe 
dient to order his reinstatement. Accordingly whilc keoping 
in view the observations made by their Lordships in A .J. R . 
1972 - S . C .- 1973 and the fact that the termination of services 
of Shri Mohinder Singh has not been shown to be justificd 
by the respondent Bank , an award is given that Shri Mohin 
der Singh , in the peculiar circumstances of the casc , should 
not be reinstated , but that he should be compensated and 
that he is ontitled to be paid pay and allowances admissiblo 
to him from the date of passing of the impugned order till 
the date this award is published . 
No order as to costs . 

P . P . R . SAWHNY , Presiding Officer . 


One of the. pleas taken by the respondent Bank is that 
since this was a case of discharge simplicitor which was per 
missible under law i.e . under various awards and Bipartito 
settlement and that they were justified and competent to yet 
rid of services of Shri Mohinder Singh , armed guard this 
Tribunal had no jurisdiction as held in 1969 - 11 -LLJ.- 799 
Supreme Court. 


It is unnecessary to go into this question in detail as it has 
already bcen found that the respondent Bank had differed 
from the findings of the enquiry officer without giving any 
reasons whatsoever and that those orders were not sustain 
able in the eyes of law . 


In this connection it may however hc stated that in the 
show causc notice and in the orders passed by the punish 
ing authority and the appellate authority the words " Services 
of Shri Mohinder Singh are terminuted ” appcar. The respon 
dent Bank has all the same inaintained that it was not a case 
of termination of services but that of discharge simplicitor.

Another pica that has been taken by them is that should 
it be found that the termination of services of Shri Mohinder 
Singh was justified he may be awarded compensation as Shri 
Mohinder Singh had forfcited their confidence and that he 
was not cntitled to reinstatement. 


15 . A plea has also been taken by the workman that the 
order of the appellate authority was not a seeking order. It 
is, hardly necessary to go into this question at it has already 
been found that nither the order of the punishing authority 
nor that of the appellate authority WAS justified inasmuch 
as they had not given any reasons for coming to a clifferent 
finding from the one given by the enquiry officer. It may 
be added that order Ext. R / 12 of the punishing authority 
is not even shown to have been served upon Shri Mohinder 
Singh , 

Having regard to the observations made above it is held 
that there was no justification for the respondent Bank to 
bave terminated the services of Shri Mohinder Singh with 
eflect from 8th May, 1968 .

On bchall of Employers - Sri Bimalendu Dutta , Advocate . 

On behalf of Workmen -- Absent. 
State : West Bengal. 

Industry : Coul Mino. 


2 . In this case the management filed its statement of caso 
on 25 - 5 - 1972 . One Robin Chatterjee , General Sccretary for 
and on behalf of the Colliery Mazdoor Sabha, CITU , Rani 
ganj, filed a memorandum wherein Robin Chatterjee , who 
signed the memorandum has been authorised by Colliery Maz 
door Sabha to represent the workmen in this proceeding . 
This so called letter of authorisation is neither fish nor flesh 
nor good red herring. Section 36 ( 1)(a ) of the Industrial 
Disputes Act and Rule 36 Form F of the Central Rules 
entitles a workman to get him represented by a registered 
trade union through one of its office bearer or members of 
the Executive provided he makes and subscribes a letter of 
authority in the form prescribed in favour of a registered 
trude union . That has not been done in this proceeding . The 
Colliery Mazdoor Sabha , CITU , P . O . Raniganj, Dist. Burdwan 
has been found by this Tribunal to be an unregistered trade 
linion that had committed fraud upon the Statutes when its 
so called General Secretary purporting to represent the work 
man member of that unregistered organisation passing by 
the name of Collicry Mazdoor Sabha, P . O . Raniganj, District 
Burdwan , raised the dispute beforc the conciliatory authority 
in flagrant violation of Section 36 ( 1) of the I. D . Act read 
with Rule 36 form F of the Central Rules . The conciliatory 
authority took the so called organisation as registered trade 
union but in Reference Case Nos . 95 and 103 of 1971 it has 
been definitely found that the so called trade union passing 
by the name of Colliery Mazdoor Sabha , CITU , P . O . Rani 
padj, Dist. Burdwan is not a registered trade union according 
to law . Therefore , the act of the so called General Secre 
tary of a unregistered trade union in raising the dispute 
before the conciliatory authority was a fraudulent action 
that vitiated the entire conciliation proceeding. There 
was no representation of the workman citbor by a registered 
tra le union authorised in that behalf by its workmen members 
to lay the charter of demand relating to the dispute before 
the authority of the management since the Colliery Mazdoor 
Sabha , P . O . Raniganj, Dist. Burdwan had never been a regis 
tered trade union and the workmen could be members of a 
trade union passing by the name of Colliery Mazdoor Sabha 
which has never been a registered trade union . So , from 
the stage of laying the charter of Jemand before the manage 
ment relating to the dispute under reference to the stage of 
conciliation proceeding, the workmen had not cither laid 
the charter of demand before the authority of the manage 
ment or before the conciliatory authority nor the workmen 
through its lawful representative laid either the charter of 
lemand before the authority of the management or before 
the conciliatory authority . 


AWARD 


By Order No. L / 1912 / 156 / 71 -LRII, dated 14th February , 
1972, the Government of India , in the Ministry of Labour 
and Rehabilitation , Department of Labour and Employment , 
referred the following dispute existing between the employers 
in relation to the management of Ghusick Colliery of Messrs 
Coal and Mineral Syndicate Private Limited and their work 
nen , to this tribunal, for adjudication, namely : 


" Whether the action of the management of Ghusick 

Colliery of Messrs Coal und Mineral Syndicate 
Private Limited , Post Office Kalipahari, District 
Burdwan in laying off of their workmen from the 
2nd September, 1971 to the 9th October, 1971 is 
legal and justificd ? If not, to what relief are the 
concerned workmen entitled ? " 


3. Therefore, the dispute referred to for adjudication lost 
its churacier as an induistrial dispute within Section 2(k ) of 
the Industrial Disputes Act. On the date of hearing neither 
Robin Chatterjee nor the workman tuned up . Having regard 
to the factual and legal position discussed above , the dispute 
referred to for adjudication being not an industrial dispute 
under Section 2 (k ) of the Industrial Disputes Act, this tri 
bunal has acquired no jurisdiction either to entertain or to 
adjudicate upon the dispute . Hence the reference is rejected . 

This is my award . 


2 . The management submitted its statement of casc on 
21- 3 - 72. The Union , Colliery Mazdoor Sabha , P . O . Rapiganj,

It may be noted that there is no provision in section 9

22 . The last submission of Mr. P . K . Bosc was that the 
concerned workmen were not members of the sponsoring 
union on the date when the industrial dispute was raised bet 
ween the parties and hence the present reference is bad in 
law . Shri Bosc drew my attention to section 36 of the In 
dustrial Disputes Act and contented that it has been admitted 
by W . W . 3 that he did not pay his subscription to the union 
after 1969 . According to Shri Bose he and other workmen 
could have raised the dispute through the union only if they 
had authorised the union to do so on a prescribed form . It 
was held by the Supreme Court in Western India Watch Co . 
versus Western India Watch Co . Workers Union and others 
( 1970 ( 3 ) Lab . I. C . p . 1033 at page 1035 ) as follows : " It 
follows, therefore , that, though a dispute may initially be an 
individual dispute, the workmen may make that dispute as 
their own , that is to say espouse it on the ground that they 
have a community of interest and are directly and substantially 
interested in the employment , non - employment , or conditions 
of work of the concerned workmen . This promise pre -sup 
poses that though at the date when the Cuuse of the dispute 
arises that dispute is an individual dispute , such a dispute can 
become an industrial dispute if it is espoused by the workmen 
or a substantial section of them after the cause of tho dis 
pute c. g ., dismissal has taken place . It inay be that at the 
date of such dismissal there is no union or that the workmen 
are not sufficiently organised to take up the causc of the con 
cerned workman and no espousal for that or any other rea 
son takes place at the time when such cause occurs. But 
that cannot mean that because there was no such union in 
cxistence on that date , the dispute cannot become an indus. 
trial one if it is taken up later on by the union or by a subs. 
tantial scction of the workmen ." 

23 . In view of the abovc decision of the Supreme Court 
there is no force in Shri Bose s contention . For the reasons 
mentioned above it must be held that the action of the man 
agement in stopping the concerned workmen from their res 
pective work with effect from the dates mentioned against 
their names in the schedulc was not justified and the workmen 
must be deemed to have bcen in continuous service of the col 
Jiery till the right, titic and interest of the owners of the col. 
liery vested absolutely in the Central Government under sec 
tion 4 of thc Coking Coal Mines (Nationalisation ) Act, 1972 
with effect from 1 - 5 -1972. 

24 . Now I come to the question of rclick. Since the right, 
title and interest of the old management of the colliery ceased 
to exist with effect from 1 - 5 -72 und vested in the Central 
Governnient and since the workmen have been held to have 
continued in their service , it is only the Bharat Coking Coal 
Ltd ., which can reinstato them in service . 

25. Shri S . S. Mukherjee appearing for Bharat Coking Coal 
Ltd ., contended that on the date the industrial dispute is said 
to have arisen or on the date when the present reference 
was made by the Central Government, there was no rela 
tionship or employer and employees between the Bharat Cok 
ing Coal Ltd ., and the workpien and hence the Bharat Coking 
Coul Ltd ., are not concerned with the dispute . Shri Mukher 
jee drew my attention to section 9 of the Coking Coal Mines 
(Nationalisation Act, 1972 , the relevant portion of which runs 
as follows: 
" 9 (i) Every liability of the owner, agent, manager , or 

managing contractor of a coking coal mine or coko 
oven plant, in relation to any period prior to the 
appointed day , shall be the liability of such owner , 
agent, manager or managing contractor , as the caso 
may be , and shall be enforceable against him and not 
against the Central Government or the Government 
Company. 


26 . The Bharat Coking Coal Ltd ., being the successor in 
interest of the South Jharia Colliery , on the principle lait 
down by the Supreme Court in Bihar State Road Transpoſt 
Corporation and State of Bihar and another (reported in 
1970 ( II) L . L .J . p . 138 ) neccssny direction for reinstate 
ment and payment of back wages shall have to be passed 
against the Bharat Coking Coal Ltd . The facts of the case 
before the Supreme Court just cited above were as follows : 
" The Government of Bihar was conducting through one of its 
departments , called the Rajya Transport Authority , an under 
taking of road transport in the State . The concerned work 
men was uppointed on 27 -7 -56 by the said authority . On 
April 20 , 1959 , the State Government set up us from May 1 , 
1959 a Corporation known as Bihar State Road Transport 
Corporation to exercise all the powers und perform all the 
functions which were till then being exercised and performed 
by the Rajya Transport Authority . The said authority passed 
an order dąted February 8 , 1959 terminating the services of 
the concerned workman. An industrial dispute having beca 
raised , it was referred to the Labour Court by an order 
dated February 24 , 1961, implcachg the Corporation as one 
of the parties to the reference. The Labour Court held that 
the order of dismissal was not justificd and concluded that the 
concerned workman was deemed to jave continued in the 
service of Rajya Transport and thereafter of the Corporation 
as the successor in title of the Raiva Transport and on this 
basis directed the Corporation to reinstatc the concerned 
workman in his service and pay compensation from February 
to September, 1959. The Corporation , thereupon, filed an ap 
plication under Article 226 of the Constitution before the 
Patna High Court contending inter alia , that the services of 
the concerned workman having been terminaled before the 
Corporation was set up , his remedy lay against the Rajya 
Transport and not against the Corporation . The Writ peti 
tion was reiected by the Putna High Court. On Appeal the 
Supreme Court substantially upheld the decision of the LA 
bour Court. The Supreme Court treated the Corporation is 
the successor in interest of the Rajya Transport authority and 
held that the concerned workman , in the absencc of any valid 
termination of his service, continued to be in the service of 
the Corporation since May 1 , 1959 and that therefore , the 
Corporation was bound to pay his wages with all emolu 
ments from May 1, 1959. The Supreme Court further held 
that for the period from February to April 1959 the Rajya 
Transport Authority was liable to pay his wages and other 
emolunients, but the Corporation successor -in -interest of the 
said authority became liable to pay wages for the period from 
February to April , 1959 and not from February to September . 
1959 as directed by the Labour Court. 


27. Shri ş . S . Mukherjee tried to distinguish tho caso of 
Bihar State Road Transport Corporation by contending that 
in that case Rajya Transport and the Corporation set up by 
the Bihar Government were the limbs of the same authority 
and hence that kecision is distinguishable . I am afraid . I 
cannot agree to this contention. The principle of law laid 
down by the Supreme Court in the case of Bihar State Road 
Transport Corporation is fully upplicable in the instant case. 
The right title and interest of South Jhula Colliery have vest 
ed in the Bharat Coking Coal Ltd ., with effect from 1st May, 
1972 by virtue of section 4 of the Coking Coal Mines (Na 
tionalisation ) Act, 1972. Section 17 ( 1 ) of the said Act lays 
down that every person who is a workin within the mean 
ing of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947, and has been , im 
mediately before the 1st of May , 1972 in the employment of 
a coking coal mine shall become on and from the appointed 
day an employee of the Government company. In the pre 
sent case , Bharat Coking Coal Ltd ., in which the right, title 
and interest of the colliery have vested and shall hold ser 
vice in the mine on the same terms and conditions and with 
the same rights to pension , gratuity , all other matters 13 
would have been admissible to him if the rights in respect of 
such & iinc had not vestcd in the Gorernment company 
As it has been found by me that the workinen had been

उपाअद्ध अनसूची म विदिष्ट विषयों के बारे म मैसर्स 
गुरू चरण राम एण्ड संस , अभूक खान मालिक डाकघर झमरीतलैया , 
जिला हजारीबाग की खरोनिया और गौरीशंकर अभूक खानों से 
सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक औद्योगिक 
विवाद विद्यमान है , 


का . आ . 395. - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की गय है कि इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में पर्शधा प्रापरटीज 
लिमिटेड , पिपराडीह , जिला शाहाबाद (बिहार ) के प्रबन्धतंत्र से 
सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक औद्योगिक 
विवाद विद्यमान है , 


___ और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णय के लिए 
निर्देशित करना यांछनीय समझती है ; 


और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है , 


अतः, अब , औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 (1947 का 
14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा (1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा उक्त 
विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7-5 के अधीन गठित 
केन्द्रीय सरकार औद्योगिक अधिकरण, धनबाद को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 


अतः अब , औदयोगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14) 
की धारा 10 की उपधारा (1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) दारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों 
का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार एसझ्वारा उक्त विवाद को

निर्देशित करती है ।

S . O . 395 . -WHEREAS the Central Government is of 
opinion that an industrial dispute exists between the employers 
in relation to the management of Parshva Properties Limited , 
Pipradih District Shahabad (Bihar ) and their workmen in respect 
of the matters spocified in the Schedule hereto annexed ; 

AND , WHEREAS the Central Government consides it 
desirable to refer the said dispute for adjudication ; 

NOW, THEREFORE, in exercise of the powers conferred 
by clausc ( d ) of sub -section ( 1 ) of section 10 of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947) , the Central Government hereby 
refers the said dispute for adjudication to the Central Govern 
mont Industrial Tribunal (No . 2 ) Dhanbad constituted under 
section 7A of the said Act , 


New Dolhi, the 24th January, 1973

S . 0 . 396. - Whereas the Central Government is of opinion 
that an industrial dispute exists between the employers in 
relation to the management of Rajasthan Atomic Power 
Project, Post Office Anushakti ( Via -Kota ) , Rajasthan and 
their workmen in respect of the matters specificd in the Scht 
dule hereto annexed ; 

And , whereas the Central Government considers it desir 
able to refer the said dispute for adjudication ; 

Now, therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by sec 
tion 7A, and clause ( d ) of sub-section ( 1 ) of section 10, 
of the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 (14 of 1947) , the 
Central Government hereby constitutes an Industrial Tribunal 
with Shri Updesh Narain Mathur, as Presiding Officer with 
headquarters at Jaipur and refers the said dispute for adjudi 
cation to the said Industrial Tribunal.

का . आ . 400. - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में कोल प्रोडक्ट्स 
प्राइवेट लिमिटेड की विक्टरी कोलियरी ( एम . जे. ग्रूप ) हाकघर 
नूतनडांगा, जिला बर्दवान के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और 
उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक आद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णय के लिए 
निर्देशित करना बांछनीय समझती है । 

अतः अब , औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 (1947 का 14 ) 
की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1) के खण्ड ( ध ) धारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों 
का प्रयोग करते हए , केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्धारा उक्त विवाद को 
उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7- क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय सरकार 
आयोगिक अधिकरण, कलकत्ता को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निशित 
करती है ।

S . O . 401. - In exercise of the powers conferred by sub 
section ( 1 ) of section 13 of the Employces Provident Funds 
and Family Pension Fund Act, 1952 ( 19 of 1952 ) , and in 
super -session of the notilication of the Government of India 
in the late Ministry of Labour, Employment and Rehabili 
tation ( Department of Labour and Employment ) No. S . O . 
2067 dated the 21st May , 1969 , the Central Government 
hereby appoints Shri V . A . Lakshmipathy to be an Inspec 
tor for the whole of the State of Gujarat for the purposes 
of the said Act and of any Scheme framed thereunder, in 
relation to any establishment belonging to , or under the 

ontrol of the Central Government, or in relation to any 
establishment connected with a railway company , a major 
port, a mine or an oilfield or a controlled industry , or in 
relation to an establishment having departments or branches 
in more than one State .

ऐसे क्षेत्र में , जिसमें उक्त अर्धािनथम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के 
उपबंध प्रदत्त है , अस्थित को ध्यान में रखते हुए उक्त कारखाने 
कां उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5- क के अधीन उपाहणीय नियोजक 
के विशेष अभिदाय के संदाय से इस अधिसूचना के राजपत्र में 
प्रकाशन की तारीख से एक वर्ष की अवधि के लिए एतद्वारा छट 
देती है ।
